Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the seismology framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 893. The resulting operator achieves bound O(197/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_893(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 893. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^197(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to seismology theory, we decompose the problem into 871 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 964. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
